6 /**
7 * @file
8 * @brief Provides lazy function calls on arrays/views.
9 */
10
11 #pragma once
12
13 #include "./concepts.hpp"
14 #include "./layout/range.hpp"
15 #include "./macros.hpp"
16 #include "./traits.hpp"
17
18 #include <cstddef>
19 #include <utility>
20 #include <tuple>
21
22 namespace nda {
23
24 /// @cond
25 // Forward declarations.
26 template <typename F, Array... A>
27 struct expr_call;
28
29 template <class F>
30 struct mapped;
31 /// @endcond
32
33 namespace detail {
34
35 // Implementation of the nda::get_algebra trait for function call expressions.
36 template <typename... Char>
37 constexpr char _impl_find_common_algebra(char x0, Char... x) {
38 return (((x == x0) && ...) ? x0 : 'N');
39 }
40
41 } // namespace detail
42
43 /**
44 * @ingroup av_utils
45 * @brief Get the resulting algebra of a function call expression involving arrays/views.
46 *
47 * @details If one of the algebras of the arguments is different, the resulting algebra is 'N'.
48 *
49 * @tparam F Callable object of the expression.
50 * @tparam As nda::Array argument types.
51 */
52 template <typename F, Array... As>
53 constexpr char get_algebra<expr_call<F, As...>> = detail::_impl_find_common_algebra(get_algebra<As>...);
54
55 /// Specialization of nda::is_expression for nda::expr_call types.
56 template <typename F, Array... As>
57 inline constexpr bool is_expression<expr_call<F, As...>> = true;
58
59 /**
60 * @addtogroup av_math
61 * @{
62 */
63
64 /**
65 * @brief A lazy function call expression on arrays/views.
66 *
67 * @details The lazy expression call fulfils the nda::Array concept and can therefore be assigned to other
68 * nda::basic_array or nda::basic_array_view objects. For example:
69 *
70 * @code{.cpp}
71 * nda::matrix<int> mat{{1, 2}, {3, 4}};
72 * nda::matrix<int> pmat = nda::pow(mat, 2);
73 * @endcode
74 *
75 * Here, `nda::pow(mat, 2)` returns a lazy expression call object which is then used in the constructor of `pmat`.
76 *
77 * The callable object should take the array/view elements as arguments.
78 *
79 * @tparam F Callable type.
80 * @tparam As nda::Array argument types.
81 */
82 template <typename F, Array... As>
83 struct expr_call {
84 /// Callable object of the expression.
85 F f;

105 10 return f(std::get<Is>(a)[arg]...);

109 /**
110 * @brief Function call operator.
111 *
112 * @details The arguments (usually multi-dimensional indices) are passed to all the nda::Array objects stored in the
113 * tuple and the results are then passed to the callable object.
114 *
115 * If the arguments contain a range, a new lazy function call expression is returned.
116 *
117 * @tparam Args Argument types.
118 * @param args Function call arguments.
119 * @return The result of the function call (depends on the callable and the arguments).
120 */
121 template <typename... Args>
122 52722 auto operator()(Args const &...args) const {
123 52722 return _call(std::make_index_sequence<sizeof...(As)>{}, args...);
124 }

127 * @brief Subscript operator.
128 *
129 * @details The argument (usually a 1-dimensional index) is passed to all the nda::Array objects stored in the tuple
130 * and the results are then passed to the callable object.
131 *
132 * If the argument is a range, a new lazy function call expression is returned.

134 * @tparam Arg Argument types.
135 * @param arg Subscript argument.
136 * @return The result of the subscript operation (depends on the callable and the arguments).
137 */

139 10 auto operator[](Arg const &arg) const {
